Um... no, not really. I mean, I definitely don't think I've had anything like that. I try to stay pretty upbeat, you know? I wouldn't say I ever really feel down or lose interest in my hobbies. I like to keep busy and stay positive.

Um... well, I wouldn't say I have trouble relaxing, exactly. I try to be pretty easygoing most of the time. But... I guess, if I'm being completely honest, sometimes on those really long design calls for work, I do get this... I don't know, this pressure building up in my body.

Um... no, I wouldn't say I have a hard time controlling worries or anything like that. Like I said, I really try to just handle what needs handling so my family doesn't have to stress. 

As for, you know, muscle tension or sleep problems... I mean, I sleep okay most nights. And muscle tension... well, my neck and shoulders get a little tight sometimes, but I'm sitting at a computer all day, so I figure that's just poor posture, right? I try to stretch when I remember. I'm pretty sure everyone who works in tech gets a stiff neck now and then. 

I don't think it's anything out of the ordinary, but... is that the kind of thing you're looking for? I just want to be sure I'm giving you the right kind of information. I hope I'm being helpful.

Um... no, not really. I mean, I try to be pretty good about finishing what I start, especially around the house. I definitely don't want my wife thinking I'm slacking off on my end of things.

Um... well, I mean, I try really hard to keep everything running smoothly. I don't want to sound like I'm completely disorganized or anything, because I really do put a lot of effort into managing my responsibilities. I want to be someone my family and my colleagues can count on.

But, I guess... well, if I'm being completely honest, part of what finally pushed me to come in and talk to you is that I've been having a little more trouble lately keeping all the moving parts together. Between my software work, and then outside of that, I have these patent notes I've been trying to organize, and I do some prep for a local workshop, plus some gallery deadlines for my wife's art that I help her with... and just regular team decisions at work. 

I don't know, it just feels like sometimes I can't quite organize it all the way I should. I end up just starting whatever is the loudest or most urgent thing in front of me, instead of what actually matters or what I had planned to do. I really try not to let things slip, though. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, childhood... well, I was generally a really good kid. I definitely wasn't disruptive or bouncing off the walls or getting into trouble. I always liked school and really wanted to please my teachers. 

But... I guess if I really look way back, even to early grade school... there were a few things. My teachers were always very kind, and they'd write on my report cards that I was bright, which was nice. But they almost always had to add that I daydreamed a lot. 

And... well, this is a little silly to admit now, but even back then, long before I was a teenager, I had this terrible habit where I would actually finish my assignments—I really did do the work!—but then I'd somehow just completely forget to turn them in. Or I'd end up losing my homework folders altogether. 

I always felt so awful when that happened because I didn't want my teachers or my parents to think I was lazy. I really was trying my best to be a good student. I hope that's the kind of example you meant? I just want to make sure I'm answering your questions the right way.
